# Decoded: `script-results/` probes + reference manifests

_Forensic decode of the probe/manifest JSONs. Every value below is transcribed verbatim from the cited JSON file. Reference-side files were captured FROM the real ProTradingRoom app and are hard evidence; our-side `script-results/audit/*` files are snapshots of OUR app and are labelled as such — NOT reference evidence._

Generated by reading the full contents of each file with `python3`. Coverage counts are reported per file so nothing is sampled.

## Reference-side region probes (`ref-topnav`, `ref-sidebar`, `ref-presentation`)

**Producer / method.** Three files with an identical schema. Each is a DOM/computed-style probe of one region of the live reference room (`app-room#topRoomDiv`). Common top-level keys: `region`, `rootRect`, `rootClass`, `rootStyle` (85 computed props), `nodeCount`+`nodes[]` (every element in region, with CSS path, tag, class, rect, attrs, `icon`, computed `style`, and `::before`/`::after` pseudo content), `icons[]` (rendered FontAwesome glyphs w/ family+size+color+rect), `controls[]` (interactive elements w/ human `label`), `targeted[]` (probes of specific `querySelector`s incl. `matchedRules` CSS text), and `theme` (the same 85 CSS custom properties present in `ref-theme.json`). No capture timestamp is stored in these files. Rects are in device pixels at a **2027px-wide viewport** (from `rootRect` of topnav = `{x:0,y:0,w:2027,h:49}`); the sidebar is captured **off-canvas** (negative x, e.g. `x:-250`), i.e. the collapsed/hidden state.

## `docs/reference/_gap-findings-raw.json` — reference→our-app gap analysis (52 fragments, 590 gaps)

**Producer / method.** A per-fragment audit: for each of the 52 saved reference HTML fragments (`pro-room/pro-room/files/fileN.html` etc.), an `identity` string + a `gaps[]` array. Each gap has `surface` (what UI part), `status` (`mismatch` | `ok` | `missing`), `evidence` (a fileN.html:line citation of the reference markup), `ourComponent` (the Svelte file it maps to, if any), `divergence` (prose describing the difference), and `priority` (`high` | `med` | `low` | null).

**Totals:** 52 fragments · 590 gaps. Status: `mismatch`=376, `ok`=172, `missing`=42. Priority: `high`=136, `med`=193, `low`=146, `null`=115. `severity`/`type` fields are absent (always null). 545/590 gaps carry an `ourComponent` mapping.

Below is **every finding, verbatim**, grouped by fragment. Status is shown as an inline tag; `evidence` and `divergence` are the exact strings from the JSON. `ourComponent` paths are shortened to the repo-relative `web/src/...` tail where present.

### [0] `file14.html` — ChatLogsModal - Angular snapshot showing populated list with Bootstrap styling

_10 gaps._

**0. Modal wrapper structure** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte`  
evidence: file14.html:2-10: `<div id="chat-logs-modal" role="dialog" class="modal fade">` with Bootstrap modal classes  
divergence: Reference uses Bootstrap `modal fade` classes and `id="chat-logs-modal"`; ours uses custom `<Modal>` component with custom `backdrop` and `panel` classes

**1. Modal header structure** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/Modal.svelte`  
evidence: file14.html:13-21: `<div class="modal-header"><h5>Chat Logs</h5><button class="btn-close btn-close-white"></button></div>`  
divergence: Reference uses Bootstrap `modal-header`, `h5` tag, and `btn-close btn-close-white` close button; ours uses custom `header` class with `h2` tag and Icon component

**2. Modal body wrapper** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/Modal.svelte`  
evidence: file14.html:23: `<div class="modal-body">`  
divergence: Reference uses Bootstrap `modal-body`; ours uses custom `body` class

**3. Toolbar / Reload button** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte:17-19`  
evidence: file14.html:25-31: `<button class="btn btn-primary my-2">Reload Log List</button>` (plain text, no icon)  
divergence: Reference: Bootstrap `btn btn-primary my-2` button with plain text; ours: custom `.reload` class with Icon component (`<Icon name="sync" size={14} />`)

**4. List container** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte:28`  
evidence: file14.html:32: `<div class="list-group">`  
divergence: Reference uses Bootstrap `list-group` div; ours uses semantic `<ul class="list">` element

**5. List item structure** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte:30-34`  
evidence: file14.html:34-61: `<div class="list-group-item list-group-item-action">` containing 3 nested `<div>` elements with `<strong>` labels and `<i>` values  
divergence: Reference: Bootstrap `list-group-item list-group-item-action` div with nested divs; ours: semantic `<li class="entry">` with 3 `<span>` children (`.date`, `.by`, `.channel`)

**6. List item field labels and formatting** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte:31-33`  
evidence: file14.html:39-60: Date in `<strong>`, By as `<strong>By:&nbsp;</strong><i>admin@protradingroom.com</i>`, Channel as `<strong>Channel:&nbsp;</strong><i>offTopic</i>`  
divergence: Reference: separate `<strong>` for labels with `&nbsp;` separators and `<i>` for values; ours: simple text spans with no label prefix, by/channel have custom CSS styling for dimming

**7. Modal footer** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte:15 (no footer)`  
evidence: file14.html:59147-59159: `<div class="modal-footer text-center"><button class="btn btn-secondary">Close</button></div>`  
divergence: Reference: Bootstrap `modal-footer text-center` with secondary button; ours: no footer rendered (Modal only renders footer if snippet passed)

**8. Empty state handling** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **low**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/ChatLogsModal.svelte:12,22-26`  
evidence: file14.html: shows populated list with multiple log entries  
divergence: Reference snapshot shows actual populated data; ours: array is hardcoded empty and shows `<div class="empty">` with icon and 'No logs yet.' message

**9. Close button styling** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/Modal.svelte:52-60`  
evidence: file14.html:20: `class="btn-close btn-close-white"`  
divergence: Reference: Bootstrap `btn-close btn-close-white`; ours: custom `.close` class with Icon component


### [1] `file9.html` — app-user-settings-modal (General Settings)

_21 gaps._

**0. Chat Settings > Image Preview section header icon** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:388-389`  
evidence: file9.html:902-904: `<i class="fas fa-image"></i>`  
divergence: Reference uses `fa-image` icon; our component uses `fa-file` (line 389: `<Icon name="file" />`). Should be `fa-image` for consistency.

**1. Chat Settings > Always Scroll To Bottom section header icon** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:433`  
evidence: file9.html:1080-1083: `<i class="fas fa-scroll"></i>`  
divergence: Reference uses `fa-scroll` icon; our component uses `fa-file` (line 433: `<Icon name="file" />`). Should be `fa-scroll` for consistency.

**2. Chat Settings > Reduce Chatlog Memory section header icon** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:443`  
evidence: file9.html:1120-1123: `<i class="fas fa-trash"></i>`  
divergence: Reference uses `fa-trash` icon; our component uses `fa-file` (line 443: `<Icon name="file" />`). Should be `fa-trash` for consistency.

**3. Modal structure, tabs layout, form fields, buttons** — status: `ok` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:135-458`  
evidence: file9.html:1-22 (modal header/close), 24-70 (tab list with 3 nav items), 131-1186 (full modal content)  
divergence: matches

**4. App Settings > Color Theme section** — status: `ok` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:156-181`  
evidence: file9.html:84-131 (fa-palette icon, Light/Dark theme radio buttons, label text)  
divergence: matches - radio buttons with fa-palette icon, Light/Dark Theme labels

**5. App Settings > Room Layout section** — status: `ok` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:184-209`  
evidence: file9.html:134-227 (fa-columns icon, 4 layout options: left/top/right/bottom, PM logs checkbox, hr divider)  
divergence: matches - all 4 radio options present, PM logs checkbox, correct labels

**6. App Settings > Colors & Size section** — status: `ok` · priority: **high**  
ourComponent: `web/src/lib/components/modals/SettingsModal.svelte:212-249`

divergence: Reference uses form-control and btn btn-outline-light Bootstrap classes, ours uses custom ghost/test classes. divergence: Our sidebar uses semantic button elements instead of Bootstrap nav-link anchors. Reference has aria-haspopup/aria-expanded on dropdown toggle; we use native <button> with onclick. evidence: file22.html:8-29 `<h5 class="card-title m-0">` containing `<div ngbdropdown class="d-inline-block dropdown">` with `<button id="dropdownBasic1" ngbdropdowntoggle class="dropdown-toggle btn btn-outline-dark">` and empty `<div ngbdropdownmenu>`  
divergence: ScreenStage.svelte (lines 34-51) has a `.bar` with a `.toggle` button group (single/split layout toggles) using custom Icon components, NOT a dropdown. The close button is in WebcamHolder overlay (lines 137-157), not in a dropdown. MainStage has tabs (lines 85-105) instead of a dropdown header.

**3. Close button (×)** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
evidence: file22.html:30-32 `<span class="float-right p-2"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></span>`

Additionally, close is rendered inside the video overlay (absolute positioned at bottom-right), not in a header.

**4. Video element** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **med**  
evidence: file22.html:34-39 `<video autoplay="autoplay" id="webcamScreenLocalPreview" class="webcamPreviewScreen"></video>`  
divergence: WebcamHolder.svelte (line 52) renders `<video {@attach attachTrack(publisher.track)} autoplay muted playsinline></video>` with `muted` and `playsinline` attributes that file22.html lacks. ScreenStage.svelte (line 68) also uses `muted playsinline`. The id and class naming differ (file22 uses single-purpose IDs, Svelte relies on component scope).

**5. Container styling** — status: `mismatch` · priority: **low**  
evidence: file22.html:5 `.card.webcamsHolderScreen` with Bootstrap card classes; file22.html:7 `.card-body`  
divergence: Svelte components use custom CSS (ScreenStage.svelte .stage, WebcamHolder.svelte .holder/.card) with CSS custom properties (--border, --bg-elev-2, --radius) and Svelte-specific styling (no Bootstrap classes). The overall architecture is flat containers (not nested card > card-body). ScreenStage has a `.bar` (top bar with count + toggle) and `.tiles` grid; WebcamHolder has `.holder` flex container.

**6. jQuery UI resize handles** — status: `missing` · priority: **high**  
evidence: file22.html:41-72 eight `<div class="ui-resizable-handle ui-resizable-{direction}" style="z-index: 90"></div>` divs  
divergence: No equivalents in Svelte codebase. The Svelte layout uses Split.svelte (lines 25, 368-376 of [id]/+page.svelte) for resizable split panes between dock and stage, but not for floating window corners/edges. This is a fundamental architectural difference: Angular had draggable floating windows, Svelte has a fixed two-pane layout.

## `docs/reference/visual-evidence-deep/manifest.json` — visual evidence build manifest

**Producer / method.** A build manifest for the "visual-evidence-deep" comparison harness. **generatedAt: `2026-06-14T23:42:59.640Z`** (the only dated file in this set). Cross-references the 52 saved original HTML fragments against current-repo screenshots and code, and rolls them up into 13 named surface `mappings`.

**sourceCounts:** originalHtmlFragments=52, currentRepoScreenshots=21, currentFailureScreenshots=4, captureGeometryFiles=4.

**limitations (verbatim):**
- Original visuals are rendered from saved HTML fragments plus the harvested original CSS, not a new live original-app browser session.
- Current repo screenshots include the named e2e screenshot artifacts plus Playwright failure screenshots from the latest run when present.
- Cards with no current screenshot are source/code-evidence only until fresh role-specific screenshots are captured.
- Hidden modals/fragments are force-shown for inspection; that is marked as inspection rendering, not native runtime state.

**originalCss** (harvested reference stylesheet): `/private/tmp/original-app-evidence/analyzed-2026-06-14T22-21-48-994Z/resources/0003-chat.protradingroom.com_styles.0d26360b9b3e223c.css-80f1b521a71b.css`

Each mapping: `name`, source `files[]`, `currentShots[]` (e2e PNG names), `currentCode[]`, `correct` (verbatim description of the correct/reference behaviour), `ours` (verbatim state-of-our-app), and `confidence`. Full `correct` / `ours` / `confidence` text below.

#### 0. Room Shell / Full Layout

- **files:** file-1.html, file2.html, file3.html, file4.html, file6.html, important-doc.html, as-splitter.html
- **currentShots:** 01-room-loaded.png
- **currentCode:** web/src/routes/rooms/+page.svelte, web/src/lib/components/RoomTopNav.svelte, web/src/lib/components/RoomSidebar.svelte, web/src/lib/components/AlertsChatDock.svelte, web/src/lib/components/MainStage.svelte
- **correct:** Angular room shell uses top fixed Bootstrap nav, left room sidebar, angular-split/as-split areas, alerts/chat pane, and presentation area in one dense work UI.
- **ours:** Svelte room shell exists and has the same broad regions, but current screenshots/code use custom CSS/grid/button markup instead of the reference Bootstrap/as-split DOM.
- **confidence:** Strong for section existence and broad layout; exact parity must be validated live after code changes.

#### 1. Top Navbar / Volume / Reload

- **ours:** RoomTopNav component exists, but evidence points to a custom topbar implementation and likely different dropdown/nav-link/button structure.
- **confidence:** Strong for original structure from isolated nav fragments and capture JSON; current visual is from existing repo screenshot, not fresh live run.

| `script-results/audit/sidebar.json` | 350,536B | dict {surface, presenter, member, adminTargeted}; per-role node captures of our sidebar |

| `script-results/audit/chat.json` | 273,191B | dict {surface, presenter, member, adminTargeted}; our chat panel per-role |

| `script-results/audit/theme-tokens.json` | 11,403B | dict {root}; 294 CSS custom properties resolved in OUR app (superset of the 85 reference tokens) |
| `script-results/audit/notes-states.json` | 177B | dict {note, states}; note='presenter Notes-tab + note sub-tab states'; states.'tab:Notes'={groups:[], note:'tab not found'} — i.e. our Notes tab was NOT found at capture time |

_Also present in `script-results/audit/` but not JSON: `css-panes.txt` (47KB raw CSS dump of our panes)._

The per-role shape (`presenter` / `member` / `adminTargeted`) means these snapshots were taken under different simulated roles — useful for OUR RBAC audit, unrelated to the reference capture which was a single admin session. **Do not diff our `theme-tokens.json` root (294 tokens) against the reference `ref-theme.json` (85 tokens) as if the extra 209 were reference values — they are ours.**

---

## Unique evidence (exists ONLY in these files)

1. **FontAwesome glyph → codepoint mapping (rendered PUA chars).** The `::before` pseudo-content in the three region probes gives the *actual rendered* Unicode private-use char for each icon (e.g. `fa-bars` → U+F0C9, `fa-user` → U+F007, `fa-mobile` → U+F10B, `fa-volume-up` → U+F028, `fa-sync` → U+F021, `fa-check` → U+F00C, `fa-network-wired` → U+F6FF, `fa-cogs` → U+F085, `fa-archive` → U+F187, `fa-comments` → U+F086, `fa-users` → U+F0C0, `fa-cog` → U+F013, `fa-sort-alpha-down` → U+F15D, `fa-search` → U+F002, `fa-desktop` → U+F108, `fa-edit` → U+F044, `fa-folder` → U+F07B, `fa-home` → U+F015, `fa-download` → U+F019, `fa-pen` → not captured as ::before). Family `"Font Awesome 5 Free"`, weight `900` (solid). This is the ground-truth for reproducing icons without shipping the exact FA build.

2. **Label → glyph dictionary** (`ref-iconmap.json` 31 + `ref-iconmap-full.json` 42). The only place the human tooltip label of each control is bound to its FA glyph + family — the authoritative source for which icon belongs on which button (incl. modal-only buttons never in the region probes).

3. **The 85 reference CSS custom properties** (`ref-theme.json`, also embedded in each region probe). The exact ProTradingRoom palette (navbar `#0c2434`, sidebar-menu `#103d5c`, accent `#45a2ff`, presenter-area `#0f2e43`, primary `#375a7f`, plus paired light/dark theme token sets) — nowhere else captured at token granularity.

4. **Pixel-accurate rects + computed styles at 2027px viewport** for every element in topnav/sidebar/presentation — exact widths, paddings, font-size/weight/line-height, and colors (e.g. active notes tab `bg rgb(69,162,255)=#45a2ff`, Download button `bg rgb(146,213,40)`, sidebar link text weight 700, topnav bg `rgb(12,36,52)=#0c2434`). The sidebar was captured **off-canvas / collapsed** (negative x).

5. **534 distinct reference `surface`→`ourComponent` gap findings with fileN.html:line citations** (`_gap-findings-raw.json`) and the **13-surface rollup** (`visual-evidence-deep/manifest.json`) — the only mapping from reference markup lines to our Svelte components, with status + priority.

6. **Fragment SHAs + structural extraction** (52 fragments' `sha`, `appTags`, `ids`, `modalIds`, `buttons`, `inputs`) — the manifest of exactly which reference HTML fragments exist and what Angular components / element ids each contains.

---

## Honest gaps

- **No capture timestamp** on `ref-topnav/sidebar/presentation`, `ref-iconmap*`, or `ref-theme`. Only `visual-evidence-deep/manifest.json` is dated (`2026-06-14T23:42:59Z`). The region probes' capture date is unknown from these files.

- **Sidebar captured collapsed/off-canvas.** All sidebar rects have negative x (root `x:-250`), so the probe reflects the *hidden* sidebar geometry, not its expanded on-stage position. Widths/heights are valid; absolute x positions are not the visible-state positions.

- **`fa-pen` glyph codepoint not captured.** The note sub-tabs use `fas fa-pen mx-1` (icon-class present in nodes/controls) but no `::before` content row was captured for them, so the exact PUA codepoint for `fa-pen` is absent here (it is glyph-named `pen`/solid in the icon maps, but the Unicode char is a gap).

- **Thumbnails are path references, not images.** `manifest.json` `thumbnail` fields point to `original-fragments/<name>.png`; the PNG bytes are not in the JSON, and the source images live under a `/private/tmp/...` capture dir that may be gone. Same for `originalCss` / `originalCodeEvidence` paths.

- **`matchedRules` CSS text not fully transcribed above.** The region probes' `controls`/`targeted` entries each carry a `matchedRules[]` array of full CSS rule text (29/24/24 elements). The load-bearing computed values were extracted; the raw rule cascade was summarized rather than dumped rule-by-rule (it is Bootstrap boilerplate + the FA `::before` content rules already captured). If a specific selector's full cascade is needed, it is in the source JSON under `targeted[i].matchedRules`.

- **`severity` and `type` fields in gap-findings are always null** (590/590) — priority (`high/med/low/``null`) is the only ranking signal present.

- **Our-side audit files are self-captures**, not reference evidence; their per-role node dumps and the 294-token `theme-tokens.json` describe OUR app and were intentionally NOT decoded in full here per the task scope.
